### [235] [It's a matter of timescale: non-linear utility in successor features and multi-objective planning and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25723)
*Liam P. H. Mertens,Lucas N. Alegre,Florent Delgrange,Diederik M. Roijers,Ann Nowé,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出现有多目标强化学习方法（SER、ESR和后继特征）在处理多奖励信号和非线性效用时存在不足：这些方法各自在不同时间尺度上处理非线性效应对用户效用的影响，但都没有考虑到同一决策问题中可能同时存在发生在不同时间尺度上的不同效应。作者通过直觉和数值示例论证了这种情况确实存在，揭示了文献中的一个重要且非平凡的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法各自处理不同时间尺度上的非线性效用问题，但现实中同一决策问题中可能同时存在发生在不同时间尺度上的多种效应，而现有方法均无法处理这种混合情况。

Method: 作者通过一个具体的示例，从直觉层面和数值层面论证了多时间尺度效应共存于同一决策问题中的可能性，从而提出一个全新的视角。

Result: 通过示例论证了现有方法（SER、ESR和后继特征）在面临同一问题中多时间尺度非线性效应时的不足，揭示了文献中的一个显著且非平凡的研究空白。

Conclusion: 当前多目标强化学习方法无法同时处理同一决策问题中发生在不同时间尺度上的非线性效应，这构成了一个重要的开放问题。

Abstract: Time is of the essence when dealing with multiple reward signals and non-linear utility. In this paper we argue that the current main approaches in multi-objectiveRL (SER and ESR), and successor features, are insufficient. While each approach deals with non-linear effects on user utility on different timescales, none of them take into account that different effects happening on different timescales can happen within the same decision problem. We motivate that this can indeed be the case by an example, both intuitively and numerically, leading to a new perspective, and a significant and non-trivial gap in the literature.

</details>


### [236] [Are LLM-Enhanced GNNs Privacy-Safe?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25727)
*Longzhu He,Zelang Wen,Chaozhuo Li,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了LLM增强GNN的隐私风险，通过一个包含五个阶段的统一框架，在六个真实文本属性图数据集上测试了六种隐私攻击方法（针对链接、标签和成员推断三类威胁），构建了42种受害者模型配置。研究发现LLM增强GNN在提升效用的同时一致性地增加了对隐私攻击的脆弱性，语义增强放大了嵌入空间中的可攻击信号，而差分隐私虽可部分缓解风险但带来显著的效用下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM增强GNN在语义信息赋能下取得了显著性能提升，但其隐私脆弱性尚未被充分探索。现有研究缺乏对该类模型在链接推断、标签推断和成员推断等威胁下隐私风险的系统性评估，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一个五阶段统一评估框架：(1)数据集准备、(2)受害者模型训练、(3)隐私攻击、(4)风险评估、(5)防御分析。在6个真实文本属性图数据集上，结合多种LLM特征增强器与代表性GNN骨干网络构建42种受害者模型配置，应用6种隐私攻击方法，并评估差分隐私防御策略。

Result: 实验表明LLM增强GNN相比浅层文本表示基线一致性表现出更高的隐私攻击脆弱性；语义增强放大了嵌入空间中与链接、标签和成员相关的信号使其更易被推断攻击利用；差分隐私可部分缓解隐私风险但引入了显著的效用退化，凸显了隐私-效用的根本权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了对LLM增强GNN隐私风险的全面理解，并为开发更安全可信的图学习系统提供了实用见解，强调需要在隐私保护和模型效用之间寻求平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently advanced graph neural networks (GNNs) by enriching node representations with semantic information, giving rise to LLM-enhanced GNNs that achieve substantial performance gains. However, their vulnerability to privacy attacks, in which adversaries infer sensitive information from model outputs, remains largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we present a systematic evaluation of privacy risks in LLM-enhanced GNNs through a unified framework consisting of five stages: (1) dataset preparation, (2) victim model training, (3) privacy attack, (4) risk assessment, and (5) defense analysis. Specifically, we conduct experiments on six real-world text-attributed graph datasets covering diverse domains. We consider six representative privacy attack methods targeting three fundamental threats, namely link, label, and membership inference, and construct 42 victim model configurations by combining multiple LLM-based feature enhancers with representative GNN backbones. Extensive experiments show that, despite their utility improvements, LLM-enhanced GNNs consistently exhibit increased vulnerability to privacy attacks compared to shallow text representation baselines. Further analysis reveals that semantic enrichment amplifies link-, label-, and membership-related signals in the embedding space, making them more exploitable by inference attacks. Finally, we evaluate differential privacy as a defense strategy and show that, while it can partially mitigate privacy risks, it introduces significant utility degradation, highlighting a fundamental privacy-utility trade-off in LLM-enhanced graph learning. Overall, this work provides a comprehensive understanding of privacy risks in LLM-enhanced GNNs and offers practical insights for developing more secure and trustworthy graph learning systems.

</details>


### [237] [Why Does Graph Learning Fail to Fully Benefit from a Text Teacher?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25741)
*Fumiaki Kimino,Ryoma Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种结合自监督GNN预训练和交替优化（E-step语言模型/M-step GNN）的多模态模型，尽管预期能提升性能，但发现其未能充分改善预测效果。作者识别出六个因素，并通过分层实验验证了这些观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探索一种无需端到端联合训练大型语言模型和GNN即可实现跨数据集迁移的多模态GNN方法，期望通过自监督预训练和交替优化使GNN能在不同节点特征维度间直接操作。

Method: 提出一个组合模型：1) 自监督方法使GNN编码器可在不同特征维度的数据集间直接迁移；2) 交替优化方法在E-step更新语言模型模块，在M-step更新GNN模块。通过分层实验控制E-step的影响来验证假设。

Result: 组合模型未能显著提升预测性能，并识别出六个阻碍因素：外部锚点的强度-安全性权衡、E-step教师知识未直接注入GCN嵌入、M-step表示空间与E-step教师空间目标不一致、GCN传播均值化自身与邻居文本信息、余弦对齐不保证判别性坐标轴、M-step保留源侧几何的力与向E-step教师移动的力相冲突。

Conclusion: 该组合方法虽在概念上有吸引力，但存在多个深层机制层面的冲突和权衡问题，导致性能提升有限。这些发现为未来改进多模态GNN与语言模型的结合方式提供了重要的指导方向。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are widely used to represent complex interactions and relationships among entities. We investigate a multimodal model that combines two complementary ideas: a self-supervised method that enables a GNN encoder pretrained on one dataset to operate directly on another dataset with a different node-feature dimensionality, without rebuilding the model or realigning the data; and an alternating optimization method that updates a language-model module in an E-step and a GNN module in an M-step, rather than jointly training a large language model and a GNN end to end on a large graph. Despite expectations, the combined model did not sufficiently improve predictive performance. We identify six factors: (1) an external anchor in the E-step has a strength-safety trade-off: a weak anchor has little effect, whereas an overly strong anchor can damage the graph representation; (2) the knowledge of the E-step teacher is not injected directly into the GCN embedding Z; (3) the representation space constructed in the M-step is not optimized for the same objective as the E-step teacher space, resulting in a compromise representation for target classification; (4) GCN propagation averages a node's own textual information with information from its neighbors; (5) cosine alignment does not guarantee axes that are discriminative for classification, so stronger geometric alignment with the E-step text anchor need not sufficiently improve the target decision boundary or classification performance; and (6) the force that preserves the source-side self-supervised geometry in the M-step conflicts with the force that moves the representation toward the E-step teacher. We support these observations through a staged set of experiments that varies the influence of the E-step.

</details>


### [238] [A Constitutive Markov Physics-Informed Neural Operator (MPNO) for Autoregressive Stability in Transient Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25744)
*Wenpu Du,Peng Zhou,Yunlong Xia,Sinuo Xin,Congcong Zhang,Boyang Zhang,Yi Zhang,Wenzheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种构造性稳定的马尔可夫物理信息神经算子（MPNO），通过将一步演化建模为行随机传播算子，并利用声阻抗谐波均值等物理耦合边权构造归一化图拉普拉斯，从架构层面约束谱半径≤1，从而解决神经算子在强间断瞬态动力学PDE（如混凝土侵彻）中的自回归不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子（如WNO、FNO）在处理强间断瞬态动力学PDE时存在自回归不稳定性：WNO在滚动展开中发散，MeshGraphNets坍缩为零预测，而FNO仅凭涌现性稳定而非构造性保证。这种不稳定性根源在于缺乏对传播算子谱半径的结构约束，导致自回归误差指数放大。

Method: 将一步演化建模为马尔可夫（行随机）传播算子P = I - α·L̃，其中L̃是归一化图拉普拉斯。物理耦合边权（声阻抗谐波均值、接触面积、牵引幅度）编码材料界面的本构信息到非负对称邻接矩阵W。通过λ_max归一化图拉普拉斯，构造性地约束谱半径ρ(P) ≤ 1，使稳定性成为可设计的架构属性而非优化目标。

Result: 在Burgers方程和二维混凝土侵彻三个PDE上，MPNO在所有测试种子（100/135/165 m/s）上稳定滚动且误差有界；单步相对L2误差为0.7304±0.0008，优于WNO，与FNO相当但参数仅为FNO的四分之一。约20K参数下，推理速度比LS-DYNA快约10^5倍。边权公式可通过替换材料属性变量跨场景迁移。

Conclusion: MPNO通过将物理本构信息编码进图结构并从构造层面约束传播算子谱半径，实现了神经算子稳定性的架构级保证，为强间断瞬态动力学问题的长期自回归预测提供了高效、稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Neural operators applied to transient-dynamics PDEs with strong discontinuities exhibit autoregressive instability: in concrete-penetration stress-field prediction, the wavelet neural operator (WNO) diverges in autoregressive rollout, while MeshGraphNets collapse to zero predictions. WNO's instability stems from the lack of a structural constraint on the spectral radius of its propagation operator; the Fourier neural operator (FNO) is stable in these measurements but only emergently, not by construction. We propose a constitutive Markov physics-informed neural operator (MPNO) modeling one-step evolution as a Markov (row-stochastic) propagation operator. Physics-coupled edge weights (acoustic-impedance harmonic mean, contact area, and traction amplitude) encode material-interface constitutive information into a nonnegative symmetric adjacency matrix W; after normalizing the graph Laplacian L = D - W by lambda_max, the propagator P = I - alpha*L~ is constructively constrained to spectral radius rho(P) <= 1, suppressing exponential amplification of autoregressive errors. Stability is thus a designable architectural property, not an optimized loss objective. On three PDEs (Burgers and two-dimensional transverse-section concrete penetration), MPNO rolls out stably with bounded error on all test seeds at 100/135/165 m/s; the single-step relative L2 error is 0.7304 +/- 0.0008, better than WNO and comparable to FNO at about one quarter of FNO's parameters. The edge-weight formula transfers across scenarios by replacing material-property variables. With about 20K parameters, MPNO delivers roughly 10^5x inference speedup over LS-DYNA.

</details>


### [239] [Comparing Corrupted Constrained Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25745)
*Laura Iacovissi,Rabanus Derr,Robert C. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出经典数据处理不等式（DPI）在机器学习受限假设类场景下不成立，并提出一个一般化的数据处理不等式，通过超预测集合的包含条件来刻画其等价性，并给出充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数据处理不等式是统计学和机器学习（如信息瓶颈、特征学习）中的关键结果，但它假设模型类包含所有可测函数。实际机器学习问题受限于模型类（如神经网络、决策树等），因此经典DPI可能失效，需要新的理论框架来指导受限模型下的学习问题。

Method: 首先构造反例证明经典DPI在受限假设类下不成立；其次提出一般化DPI，要求联合分布的受限贝叶斯风险不超过随机修改后分布的受限贝叶斯风险；证明该不等式等价于损失函数和模型类所诱导的超预测集合（superprediction set）上的集合包含条件；最后推导该包含条件的充分条件。

Result: 证明了经典DPI在受限模型类下存在反例；建立了一般化DPI与超预测集合包含条件之间的等价关系；给出了确保该集合包含成立的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了统计学理论（DPI）与机器学习现实约束（受限假设类）之间的鸿沟，为受限学习场景下的理论保证提供了新工具，具有潜在的应用于特征学习和信息瓶颈原则的扩展。

Abstract: A key result in statistics is the data processing inequality, originally proved by Blackwell (1951) and later refined by DeGroot (1962) in terms of statistical uncertainty. It states that the Bayes risk of a statistical experiment obtained by stochastically modifying another experiment cannot be lower than the Bayes risk of the original experiment, regardless of the loss function or prior chosen. In machine learning, this result underlies applications such as the information bottleneck principle and some feature learning techniques. However, machine learning problems are constrained learning problems: the model class used does not include all measurable functions. We present a simple counterexample showing that the classical data processing inequality fails to hold in such a setting. Hence, we formulate a generalized data processing inequality, requiring the constrained Bayes risk of a joint distribution (with respect to a loss function and a constrained hypothesis class) to lower bound the constrained Bayes risk on the stochastically modified distribution, regardless of the choice of distribution. We show this inequality to be equivalent to a set containment condition on a specific function set induced by the loss and model class, called the superprediction set. Finally, we derive sufficient conditions for this containment.

</details>


### [240] [TailSFT: Filtered Fine-Tuning Improves Post-Training Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25756)
*Sadhika Malladi,Samy Jelassi,Dylan Foster,Jordan T. Ash,Akshay Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailSFT，一种简单修改监督微调的方法，通过过滤已拟合的序列，将学习集中于数据分布的尾部（未被充分建模的区域），从而产生更适合后续强化学习的模型检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练在提升推理和智能体能力方面非常有效，但它通常需要以已有强大能力的基础模型为前提。现有训练流程生成的模型可能并非最适合RL微调，需要探索如何让SFT阶段产出更适合后续RL的初始化模型。

Method: TailSFT方法：在监督微调过程中过滤掉已经拟合好的序列，使训练集中于数据分布中未被充分建模的尾部区域。通过控制实验和理论分析验证了具体过滤标准的设计选择。

Result: 在OLMo-3 7B上，TailSFT在数学和代码评估中pass@16性能提升最高可达17%绝对值；这些高覆盖率检查点在后续GRPO训练中带来最高4%绝对值的pass@1提升，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种轻量级诊断方法用于识别TailSFT最可能奏效的场景，并倡导一种分阶段意识（stage-aware）的模型开发方法，即根据中间检查点对后续训练的支撑效果来评估其质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training drives reasoning and agentic capabilities in modern AI systems, yet a growing body of work shows that it is most effective when used to fine-tune an already capable base model. We question whether existing pipelines yield models that are most suitable for reinforcement learning. Building on prior work highlighting the role of coverage and pass@K as predictors of post-RL performance, we design a simple modification to supervised fine-tuning, TailSFT, which filters out already fit sequences during training, thereby focusing learning on under-modeled regions, or the tail, of the data distribution. We justify and validate the design choices in TailSFT, particularly the specific filtering criteria, through a combination of controlled experiments and theoretical analysis. On OLMo-3 7B, TailSFT often improves pass@16 performance on math and coding evaluations, with gains up to 17% absolute, while incurring minimal computational overhead. These higher-coverage checkpoints consistently translate to up to 4% absolute pass@1 gains in subsequent GRPO runs, demonstrating that TailSFT checkpoints serve as better initializations for RL. We further introduce a lightweight diagnostic for identifying settings where TailSFT is most likely to help. More broadly, our results motivate a principled, stage-aware approach to model development, in which intermediate checkpoints are judged by how effectively they support subsequent training.

</details>


### [241] [Learning from waste: Machine Learning for health risk prediction and computer vision-based sorting in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25759)
*Hilda Adwubi Osei,Catherine Tenewaa Osei,Desdemona Yaa Asobayire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用随机森林分类器（宏F1=0.63）验证了加纳Atonsu社区生活垃圾处理方式与疾病类型之间的定量关联，同时开发了MobileNetV2图像分类模型实现自动化垃圾分类（准确率88.2%，宏F1=0.87），为资源受限环境提供低成本的视觉分类方案，并强调技术进步需配合制度支持才能实现公共健康改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固废不当处理是全球公共卫生和环境问题，加纳面临严峻挑战。此前2022年在Atonsu开展的实地研究仅通过描述性分析发现社区感知的废弃物处理与疾病模式之间的关系，缺乏定量验证。本研究旨在通过数据驱动方法提供量化证据，弥补这一空白。

Method: 采用两种数据驱动方法：(1) 随机森林分类器，利用废弃物处理实践和人口统计数据预测疾病类别，在自称患病的受访者保留组（N=69）上评估；(2) MobileNetV2图像分类模型，通过视觉识别实现自动化垃圾分类，在测试集（N=415）上评估。

Result: 随机森林模型在保留组上取得宏F1分数0.63，废弃物处理方法成为预测疾病类型最重要的实质性预测因子。MobileNetV2图像分类模型达到88.2%的准确率和0.87的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: 研究为先前仅定性记录的社区健康关联提供了定量证据，展示了自动化垃圾分类在低资源环境中的应用潜力。同时强调，仅靠技术性能无法保证公共卫生改善，有效制度支持和实施同样不可或缺。

Abstract: The inappropriate disposal of solid waste remains a significant public health and environmental concern worldwide, including in Ghana. Poor sanitation and improper waste management practices contribute to substantial economic costs and avoidable deaths annually. In 2022, a field study in Atonsu, Kumasi, Ghana, reported a community-perceived relationship between household waste disposal and illness patterns, but only through descriptive analysis without quantitative validation. This study extends that investigation using two data-driven approaches. First, a Random Forest classifier was developed to predict illness categories using waste disposal practices and demographic survey data. On a held-out group of respondents who reported illness (N=69), the model obtained a macro F1 score of 0.63, with disposal method emerging as the most important substantive predictor of illness type. Second, a MobileNetV2 image classification model enabled automated waste sorting via visual recognition, achieving 88.2% accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.87 on the test set (N=415). The vision-based approach offers an affordable, camera-driven alternative to complex multi-sensor systems, making it highly suitable for resource-constrained settings. Taken together, the findings provide quantitative evidence for a community health relationship previously documented only qualitatively. They demonstrate the potential for automated waste-sorting in low-resource environments. Importantly, the results illustrate that technological performance alone does not guarantee public health improvements; effective institutional support and implementation are equally necessary.

</details>


### [242] [Drift-Aware Multimodal User Representation Learning via Multi-Scale Temporal Modeling and Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25773)
*Ziqing Qian,Haohang Chen,Shengqi Dang,Yuhan Xiong,Canyu Shen,Jiaying Lei,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUMoE框架，用于漂移感知的多模态用户表征学习。模型包含时间动力学感知主干网络（整合静态画像、短期行为信号和长期依赖）和稀疏专家混合（MoE）兴趣适配器（通过专家特化和自适应路由解耦多种潜在兴趣），并采用三阶段训练策略以稳定优化。在真实社交媒体数据集上，DUMoE在用户兴趣预测和交互预测任务中均优于现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体用户行为噪声大且随时间动态演化，用户偏好存在兴趣漂移问题，呈现多尺度时间模式和多种并存的兴趣。现有方法难以同时建模时间动态性和多兴趣分离，因此需要统一的框架来解决这一挑战。

Method: DUMoE框架由两部分组成：(i) 时间动力学感知主干网络，捕获并整合静态画像、短期行为信号和长期依赖；(ii) 稀疏混合专家(MoE)兴趣适配器，通过专家特化和自适应路由实现多种潜在兴趣的解耦。每个专家建模一个独立的兴趣子空间，门控网络动态选择并聚合稀疏的专家子集。此外引入三阶段训练策略，解耦主干学习、专家特化和门控优化。

Result: 在真实社交媒体数据集上的大量实验表明，DUMoE在用户兴趣预测和交互预测两项任务上均一致性地优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: DUMoE通过结合时间动力学感知主干与稀疏MoE兴趣适配器，有效解决了用户兴趣漂移背景下的多模态用户表征学习问题，在预测任务上取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Understanding user preferences from noisy and temporally evolving social media behaviors is fundamentally challenging due to interest drift, where user preferences shift across time and exhibit both multi-scale temporal patterns and diverse co-existing interests. To address this, we propose DUMoE, a unified framework for drift-aware multimodal user representation learning. Our model consists of (i) a temporal dynamics-aware backbone that captures and integrates static profiles, short-term behavioral signals, and long-term dependencies into a coherent representation, and (ii) a sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) interest adapter that disentangles multiple latent interests via expert specialization and adaptive routing. Each expert models a distinct interest subspace, while a gating network dynamically selects and aggregates a sparse subset of relevant experts for each user. To enable stable and effective optimization, we further introduce a three-stage training strategy that decouples backbone learning, expert specialization, and gating optimization. Extensive experiments on real-world social media datasets show that DUMoE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on both user interest prediction and interaction prediction tasks.

</details>


### [243] [EXAONE Tabular 1.0 : Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25774)
*Moonjung Eo,Min-Kook Suh,Hye-Seung Cho,Jiwon Kim,Seoyoon Kim,Sangjun Nam,Soonyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EXAONE Tabular是一个紧凑的表格基础模型家族，通过上下文学习（in-context learning）实现分类和回归任务，无需针对数据集做梯度更新。它完全在合成结构因果模型（SCM）先验上预训练，核心贡献是重新设计了表格上下文学习的架构：在每个Transformer层中，将特征轴注意力与支持条件化的项目轴注意力交织，并通过项目摘要和特征摘要token进行调节。在四个公开基准上表现优异：TabArena上20.81M参数的分类模型总体排名第一，回归性能达到1.64B参数的TabFM水平但推理成本约为其1/11；BCCO和TALENT上分类第二、回归第一；ScoringBench上点估计和预测分布质量均取得最佳平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格基础模型通常先压缩特征为固定行嵌入，再交由独立的行级学习器处理，这种分离式设计限制了上下文学习的能力。作者旨在通过架构中心的重新设计，实现更高效、更强预测性能的紧凑型表格基础模型。

Method: 提出EXAONE Tabular模型家族，采用层次交互的Transformer架构：每个Transformer层内，特征轴注意力在处理每个项目内部，支持条件化的项目轴注意力在每个特征内部运行，两者通过项目摘要token和特征摘要token进行信息调节。模型仅在合成的结构因果模型（SCM）先验上预训练，不依赖真实数据集。

Result: 在TabArena上，20.81M参数的分类模型总体排名第一，超越调优的集成模型和4小时AutoML流水线；回归性能达到1.64B参数TabFM的水平，推理成本约为其1/11。BCCO和TALENT上分类排名第二、回归排名第一。ScoringBench上点估计与预测分布质量的R²、RMSE、CRPS评估均取得最佳平均排名。

Conclusion: EXAONE Tabular确立了作为最先进紧凑型表格基础模型家族的地位，在分类、点回归和概率回归中兼具强预测性能与高效的模型设计，证明了架构中心设计在表格上下文学习中的有效性。

Abstract: EXAONE Tabular is a compact tabular foundation model family for classification and regression via in-context learning, producing predictions without dataset-specific gradient updates. Pretrained exclusively on a synthetic structural-causal-model (SCM) prior, its central contribution is an architecture-centered redesign of tabular in-context learning. Rather than compressing features into a fixed row embedding before a separate row-level learner, EXAONE Tabular interleaves feature-axis attention within each item with support-conditioned item-axis attention within each feature at every Transformer layer, mediated by item-summary and feature-summary tokens. Across four public benchmarks, EXAONE Tabular combines strong predictive performance with high efficiency. On TabArena, its 20.81M-parameter classification model ranks first overall, surpassing tuned ensembles and 4-hour AutoML pipelines, while regression reaches the performance regime of the 1.64B-parameter TabFM at roughly 1/11 the inference cost. On BCCO and TALENT, EXAONE Tabular ranks second in classification and first in regression. On ScoringBench, it achieves the best mean rank for both point-estimation and predictive-distribution quality, leading the $R^2$, RMSE, and CRPS evaluations. Together, these results establish EXAONE Tabular as a state-of-the-art compact tabular foundation model family, combining strong predictive performance across classification, point regression, and probabilistic regression with an efficient model design.

</details>


### [244] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Aggregation in Semi-Supervised Federated Learning with non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25794)
*Rene Glitza,Luca Becker,Rainer Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出pFedMARL方法，利用多智能体强化学习（TD3）动态调整联邦学习中的聚合策略，以应对非IID数据分布和对抗客户端问题，在音频谱图Transformer上验证效果优于FedAvg、Ditto和本地训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非IID异构客户端数据分布下，全局模型会变得次优且有偏见，现有方法难以同时兼顾全局鲁棒性和客户端个性化需求。

Method: 采用服务器端智能体动态调整各客户端的聚合权重以优化全局模型鲁棒性；客户端端智能体在全局与本地更新之间进行平衡，实现个性化模型训练，且无需预训练。算法基于Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3)。

Result: 在半监督音频谱图Transformer任务中，pFedMARL在多个非IID场景及存在对抗客户端的情况下，匹配或超越FedAvg、Ditto和本地训练方法，显著提升准确率、鲁棒性和公平性。

Conclusion: pFedMARL通过将FL聚合策略建模为多智能体强化学习问题，能够主动提升联邦学习的准确率、鲁棒性和公平性，适用于真实世界的部署场景。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed training of machine learning models while preserving data privacy. However, FL struggles with heterogeneous, non-IID client data distributions, resulting in sub-optimal and biased global models. In this paper, we propose pFedMARL, a novel approach leveraging Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) with Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to dynamically adapt aggregation strategies in FL settings. Our method employs a server-side agent adjusting client contributions to optimize global model robustness and client-side agents balancing global and local updates to personalize models effectively without pre-training. We demonstrate superior performance of pFedMARL for training a semi-supervised audio spectrogram transformer, matching or outperforming FedAvg, Ditto, and local training approaches across multiple non-IID scenarios and in the presence of adversarial clients. Our results indicate that pFedMARL actively improves accuracy, robustness, and fairness, making it suitable for real-world deployments.

</details>


### [245] [Geometry-Constrained Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: Learning Edge Geometry via Banach Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25807)
*K S Sesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何约束KAN（geometry-constrained KANs），通过在每个边上学习一个标量指数p>1来控制激活函数的几何特性，从而替代固定基函数（样条、多项式、傅里叶特征）的KAN变体。实验表明该方法在符号回归基准上匹配或超越所有固定基基线，尤其在噪声和少样本场景下表现突出，且学习到的指数提供了可解释的几何信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KAN变体依赖固定的基函数（样条、多项式、傅里叶特征），这些固定基在数据观测前就强加了一种函数空间几何，限制了模型的适应性。作者希望让几何本身成为可学习的参数，而不是事前固定。

Method: 从Banach对偶映射出发推导出一族边缘激活函数，每个边引入一个可学习的标量指数p>1来控制几何特性。p<2时产生类似ℓ1（LASSO）的尖锐阈值行为，p=2恢复线性区域，p>2产生原点附近的更平坦响应。指数p通过训练学习，从而实现了数据驱动的几何自适应。

Result: 在50个符号回归目标（40个AI Feynman基准+10个合成压力测试）上：Banach-KAN中位NRMSE为0.030，与Chebyshev并列最优；在18方程核心集上平均排名最佳（2.00）；噪声鲁棒性突出——ℓp-KAN在σ从0到1时仅退化3.7倍（未正则化样条退化21.6倍）；少样本场景下Banach-KAN获胜次数最多；学习到的指数提供可解释的目标依赖几何排序。

Conclusion: 几何约束KAN通过可学习的Banach指数实现了自适应几何，在精度、噪声鲁棒性和少样本泛化上均优于或匹敌固定基KAN，同时提供了可解释的几何信号，是KAN架构中一种有前景的参数化方案。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) replace fixed activations in deep architectures with learnable univariate edge functions, making the choice of edge parametrisation central. Existing variants rely on fixed bases such as splines, polynomials, or Fourier features, which impose a function-space geometry before data are observed. We introduce geometry-constrained KANs, a family of edge activations derived from Banach duality maps in which the geometry itself is learned through a scalar exponent $p > 1$ per edge. This exponent controls the qualitative response: sub-Euclidean values produce sharp, threshold-like behaviour reminiscent of the $\ell_1$ (LASSO) geometry, $p = 2$ recovers the linear regime, and larger values produce flatter responses near the origin. Across 50 symbolic-regression targets ($40$ from the AI Feynman benchmark plus $10$ synthetic stress tests), geometry-constrained KANs match or beat every fixed-basis baseline on median NRMSE (Banach-KAN $0.030$, tying Chebyshev and improving on splines); on average rank Banach-KAN is best on the $18$-equation core ($2.00$) and statistically tied with the strongest spline on the full benchmark ($2.32$ vs. $2.34$). The clearest gains appear under measurement noise: as $σ$ grows from $0$ to $1$, $\ell^p$-KAN degrades only $3.7\times$ -- below even a cross-validated spline ($\approx 11\times$) -- while an unregularised spline degrades $21.6\times$; Banach-KAN degrades $8.8\times$, comparable to a tuned spline but far more stable than the unregularised one. Banach-KAN also takes the most per-equation wins in the small-sample regime, with fixed-basis models catching up only as the training set grows. Learned exponents provide an interpretable, relative signal: at a fixed initialisation they reveal a consistent, target-dependent geometric ordering across equation families and input dimensions.

</details>


### [246] [Canalization Before Generalization: Grokking as a Dynamical Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25813)
*Yiming Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过在学习突现（grokking）的早期阶段施加短期、固定时长的权重衰减（WD）脉冲，研究网络如何从众多等价解中选择泛化解。研究发现，权重衰减脉冲对泛化时间的影响在平台期早期无序，但后期形成稳定的剂量排序（更强的WD加快泛化，更强的WD减缓泛化），且这种排序在可见泛化出现之前就形成。同时，测试损失障碍在扰动与基线泛化检查点之间坍缩至零，而排序效应持续存在，作者将此命名为"函数选择的运河化"（canalization of function selection）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过度参数化神经网络中存在大量能拟合训练数据但在未见样本上表现不同的解，理解训练过程中如何选择出具有良好泛化能力的解是核心问题。Grokking现象将训练拟合与可见泛化分离，为研究这一选择过程提供了独特窗口。作者希望探究训练过程中解选择的时间动态，以及外部扰动（如权重衰减）如何影响这一选择轨迹。

Method: 作者在三个grokking任务上，于泛化平台期的不同时间点施加短期、固定持续时间的权重衰减脉冲（包括增强和减弱两种方向），系统测量这些脉冲对后续泛化时间的影响，并通过测试损失障碍（test-loss barriers）来分析基线泛化解与扰动后解之间的函数差异。

Result: 结果显示：（1）权重衰减脉冲对泛化时间的影响在平台期早期无序，后期形成稳定的剂量排序；（2）这种剂量排序在三个任务中均在可见泛化出现之前就建立；（3）扰动后与基线的泛化检查点之间的测试损失障碍坍缩至零，表明解空间被压缩到单一函数，但剂量排序的时间敏感性仍然持续。

Conclusion: 训练过程中函数选择经历了从"开放"到"运河化"的转变：早期解选择尚未被约束，对扰动的响应无序；后期训练轨迹被约束在狭窄的解决方案通道内，泛化函数基本确定（障碍为零），但训练动力学对扰动的时间敏感性仍然保留。这种"函数选择的运河化"机制为理解grokking和解选择提供了新的视角。

Abstract: For overparameterized neural networks, many solutions can fit the training data equally well while behaving very differently on unseen samples. Grokking separates training fit from visible generalization, providing a window for studying how this selection develops during training. We sweep short, fixed-duration weight-decay (WD) pulses across this plateau and measure how they shift later generalization time. Across three grokking tasks, these shifts are unordered early in the plateau but later form a stable dose ordering, with stronger WD increases leading to earlier generalization and stronger WD decreases leading to later generalization. This ordering emerges before visible generalization in all three tasks. Meanwhile, test-loss barriers between perturbed and baseline generalization checkpoints collapse toward zero while the ordered timing effects persist. We call this combination of increasingly constrained solution selection and persistent dose-ordered timing sensitivity the canalization of function selection.

</details>


### [247] [Learning Continuous Regional Temperature Fields with Lead-Time and Resolution Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25823)
*Chunlei Shi,Jiong Wang,Yi-Lin Wei,Junming Hou,Jinjin Liu,Yecheng Zhang,Dan Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSTF（连续时空温度预报器），将区域近地面温度(T2M)预报建模为查询条件化的连续时空场评估问题。通过将空间位置、预报时效和输出分辨率作为显式查询，CSTF突破了传统深度学习预报器输出固定的限制，实现任意时效、任意分辨率的温度场预报。实验表明该方法在东南中国0-6小时ERA5-Land基准上取得最佳综合确定性技能，Bias降低17.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的区域天气预报器只能在预设网格上生成固定数量的未来帧，当预报产品需要在查询相关的时效或显示分辨率下评估时受到限制，难以满足灵活的用户需求。

Method: CSTF首先将多变量ERA5历史数据编码为潜在气象状态，然后以坐标场方式解码T2M。将空间位置、预报时效和输出分辨率作为查询条件，实现统一场评估框架内的逐小时预报、中间时效诊断和分辨率可控输出。同时设计空间梯度、时间差分和尺度一致性三种正则化目标，以保持灵活场查询下的连贯性。

Result: 在东南中国0-6小时ERA5-Land基准上，CSTF取得最佳综合确定性技能，包括Bias降低17.0%；全球范围诊断进一步展示了灵活的时效和分辨率可控推理能力。

Conclusion: 将预报视为查询条件化的连续场评估是突破固定输出限制的有效途径，CSTF通过神经场框架统一了时效、分辨率和空间位置的灵活查询，显著提升了区域T2M预报的适应性与准确性。

Abstract: Accurate regional near-surface temperature forecasting is fundamental to short-range weather services and downstream risk assessment. Existing deep learning-based regional forecasters commonly produce a fixed set of future frames on a prescribed grid, limiting their use when forecast products must be evaluated at query-dependent lead times or display resolutions. To overcome these fixed-output constraints, we formulate regional T2M forecasting as query-conditioned continuous spatiotemporal temperature field evaluation and propose the Continuous Spatiotemporal Temperature Forecaster (CSTF), a neural field that turns forecast lead time and output resolution into explicit queries when evaluating 2-m temperature (T2M). Specifically, CSTF first encodes multivariable ERA5 histories into latent meteorological states and then decodes T2M as a coordinate-based field. Accordingly, spatial location, forecast lead time, and output resolution are introduced as queries, enabling standard hourly forecasts, intermediate lead-time diagnostics, and resolution-controllable outputs within a unified field-evaluation framework. Furthermore, to maintain coherence across flexible field queries, we design spatial-gradient, temporal-difference, and scale-consistency objectives that regularize regional thermal structures, lead-wise evolution, and cross-resolution agreement. Experiments on the Southeast China 0-6 h ERA5-Land benchmark demonstrate that CSTF achieves the best aggregate deterministic skill, including a 17.0 percent reduction in Bias, with global-scope diagnostics further illustrating flexible lead-time and resolution-controllable inference.

</details>


### [248] [VINCENT: Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25841)
*Fan-Sheng Chuang,Xuchen Li,Yujing Bian,Kaixiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VINCENT框架，用于药物协同预测的可解释性分析。该框架从固定预测器中提取原子级证据，将原子分组成化学上一致的基序，并通过重复局部扰动验证基序对，再反馈验证结果以细化基序分配，最终生成化学一致、扰动稳定且与预测器行为对齐的跨药物基序对解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物协同预测中，单一的协同分数不足以满足研究需求，研究者还需要知道哪些分子区域共同驱动了预测结果。现有可解释模型基于原子或子结构级信号，但解释被固嵌入预测器架构中，且没有在重复扰动下验证跨药物区域分数，也没有将验证证据反馈回解释过程。因此需要一种化学一致、扰动稳定且与预测器行为对齐的可靠的基序对解释方法。

Method: VINCENT是一个训练后(post-training)框架，面向固定的交互感知协同预测器。其流程包括：(1) 从注意力与梯度信号中提取原子对证据；(2) 将原子分组成化学上一致的基序（motif）；(3) 通过重复局部扰动验证候选基序对；(4) 将验证证据反馈以细化基序分配，迭代产生满足化学一致性、扰动稳定性和预测器行为对齐三大准则的解释。

Result: 在25对文献标注的子集上，VINCENT的平均基序召回率(motif recall)达到0.826（95% CI: 0.78-0.87），优于基线方法的0.49-0.66。在全部71对测试组合上，其验证后的交互分数实现了3.36的TP/TN分离度。结果表明闭环扰动验证比现有替代方法更准确地恢复了文献支持的分子区域，同时产出的跨药物交互分数更好地反映了预测器行为。

Conclusion: 闭环扰动验证框架（VINCENT）能够比现有方法更准确、更可靠地恢复文献支持的分子基序对，其解释不仅化学一致且扰动稳定，还能更好地与预测器行为对齐，为药物组合发现提供了更可信的分子层面解释工具。

Abstract: Drug synergy prediction estimates whether two drugs produce a stronger joint effect than expected from their individual activities. For drug combination discovery, a single synergy score is often not enough: researchers also need to know which molecular regions jointly drive the prediction. We study motif-pair synergy explanation, which identifies pairs of chemically coherent regions, one from each drug, that jointly contribute to predicted synergy. Existing interpretable synergy models expose atom- or substructure-level signals, but their explanations are built into the predictor architecture, and none validates cross-drug region scores under repeated perturbations or feeds that evidence back to refine the explanation. A reliable motif-pair explanation should instead be chemically coherent, perturbation-stable, and aligned with predictor behavior. We introduce VINCENT (Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics), a post-training framework for a fixed interaction-aware synergy predictor. VINCENT extracts atom-pair evidence from attention and gradient signals, groups atoms into chemically coherent motifs, and validates candidate motif pairs through repeated local perturbations. The validated evidence is fed back to refine motif assignments, yielding explanations that satisfy these three criteria. On a 25-pair literature-annotated subset, VINCENT achieves a mean motif recall of 0.826 (95% CI: 0.78-0.87), compared with 0.49-0.66 for baselines. Across all 71 test pairs, its validated interaction scores yield a TP/TN separation of 3.36. These results show that closed-loop perturbation validation recovers literature-supported molecular regions more accurately than existing alternatives while producing cross-drug interaction scores that better reflect predictor behavior.

</details>


### [249] [CEDAR: Controlled and Event-Driven Demand Forecasting via Residual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25871)
*Junjie Meng,Ranxu Zhang,Zi-an Zhang,Shujun Liu,Xiaoning Qi,Xiaozhou Xu,Yanyong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CEDAR，一个两阶段的决策条件仿真框架，用于大型电商市场中的可控需求预测。第一阶段使用Action-Interleaved Transformer学习可控的动作条件状态转移，第二阶段使用残差校正模块利用外部事件信号和LLM辅助文本表示来修正事件驱动的偏差。在阿里1688约3200万条产品轨迹的数据集上，离线实验和在线实验均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测方法是被动的，即使将操作决策作为协变量纳入，仍优化基于历史策略的相关性外推，存在自回归惯性，并将内生市场演化与决策诱导转移混淆，导致策略不敏感的滚动预测和不可靠的反事实分析。而电商平台中的规划任务（如商家评估预算安排下的销售结果）需要决策条件的仿真能力。

Method: CEDAR采用两阶段框架：Stage I通过Action-Interleaved Transformer学习可控的动作条件状态转移，用于在计划干预下进行滚动预测；Stage II通过Residual Correction Module利用外部事件信号和LLM辅助的文本表示，将嘈杂的事件描述与产品上下文对齐，并修正事件驱动的偏差。

Result: 在阿里1688约3200万条产品轨迹的大规模真实数据集上，离线实验和在线生产环境的受控实验均表明CEDAR在仿真精度上持续优于强时间序列预测基线，并为实际预算规划带来了实际收益。

Conclusion: CEDAR通过显式建模动作条件转移和事件偏差校正，弥合了被动预测与主动决策条件仿真之间的差距，为电商大规模市场中的预算规划等下游任务提供了更可靠的仿真基础。

Abstract: Forecasting in large-scale e-commerce marketplaces is increasingly required to support planning: merchants need to evaluate sales outcomes under future action sequences such as budget schedules, rather than passively predicting what happens next. However, most existing time series forecasting (TSF) approaches remain inherently passive. Even when incorporating operational decisions as auxiliary covariates, they typically optimize for correlation-based extrapolation under historical policies. This design suffers from autoregressive inertia and conflates endogenous market evolution with decision-induced transitions, leading to policy-insensitive rollouts and unreliable counterfactual analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose CEDAR (Controlled and Event-Driven Demand forecasting via Action-aware Residual decomposition), a two-stage framework for robust decision-conditioned simulation. In Stage I, an Action-Interleaved Transformer learns controllable action-conditioned state transitions for rollout under planned interventions. In Stage II, a Residual Correction Module leverages external event signals and LLM-assisted text representations to align noisy event descriptions with product context and correct event-driven deviations. Our study is enabled by a large-scale real-world dataset from Alibaba 1688, comprising approximately 32 million product trajectories with paired state-action sequences and aligned event signals. Extensive offline experiments and online controlled experiments in production demonstrate that CEDAR consistently improves simulation accuracy over strong TSF baselines and delivers practical gains for real-world budget planning.

</details>


### [250] [How Edge of Stability Hinders SCAFFOLD in Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25873)
*Anant Khandelwal,Michael Crawshaw,Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了联邦学习中SCAFFOLD算法在实际中不如FedAvg的原因：在边缘稳定性（EoS）和渐进锐化现象下，SCAFFOLD对全局梯度的估计能力严重退化，导致其理论优势无法在实践中体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中异构数据理论上会减慢优化，为此设计了SCAFFOLD等对数据异构不敏感的算法。然而尽管SCAFFOLD有很强的理论保证，在实践中却通常不如简单的FedAvg。作者希望探究这一理论与实践的差距背后的原因。

Method: 作者通过广泛的实证探测来研究联邦优化中的EoS动力学。具体包括：(1) 在多种架构和超参数下观察FedAvg和SCAFFOLD的EoS行为；(2) 测量锐度平衡值与学习率及数据异构程度的关系；(3) 分析锐度与SCAFFOLD全局梯度估计误差之间的相关性。

Result: 研究发现：EoS动力学在FedAvg和SCAFFOLD中均普遍存在；锐度平衡值与学习率成反比（与GD一致），且数据异构程度（而非本地步骤数）也会影响平衡值；SCAFFOLD在EoS处对全局梯度的估计能力严重退化，锐度与SCAFFOLD的全局梯度估计误差高度相关。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD在深度学习中的糟糕表现源于：在EoS处的高锐度条件下，SCAFFOLD无法可靠地估计全局梯度，这为理解联邦优化算法理论与实践之间的差距提供了新的机制性解释。

Abstract: In federated learning, it is well known that heterogeneous data can (in theory) slow down optimization, and much effort has been directed at designing optimization algorithms that are unaffected by data heterogeneity, such as the SCAFFOLD algorithm. Yet, despite strong theoretical guarantees, SCAFFOLD does not usually outperform the much simpler FedAvg in practice. In this work, we propose that this gap is due to the presence of Edge of Stability (EoS) and progressive sharpening in federated optimization, supported by extensive empirical probing. First, we find that EoS-like dynamics occur with both FedAvg and SCAFFOLD under a variety of architectures and hyperparameters. We observe that the equilibrium value of the sharpness is inversely proportional to the learning rate (as in GD), and interestingly, the degree of data heterogeneity (but not the number of local steps) also affects the equilibrium value. Most importantly, we observe that SCAFFOLD's ability to estimate the gradient of the global objective is severely degraded at the EoS, as measured by the correlation between sharpness and SCAFFOLD's error in estimating the global gradient along the optimization trajectory. This suggests a mechanism for SCAFFOLD's lackluster performance in deep learning: with high sharpness at the EoS, SCAFFOLD cannot reliably estimate the global gradient.

</details>


### [251] [One Symptom, Three Levers: A Critical Review of On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25936)
*Justin Robert,Raheel Qader*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)的综述性论文，聚焦于数学推理领域中的"坍缩"（collapse）问题。OPSD通过让模型自身充当教师（利用特权信息），在消除独立教师模型成本的同时，也引入了偏差。论文将坍缩视为由三个杠杆控制的症状：(i)信号应用位置（token加权方式）；(ii)教师所见的特权信息性质；(iii)信号变化时机（教师动态与引导衰减）。文章未报告新实验，贡献在于提供统一术语框架，并厘清已解决与仍有争议的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD方法虽然早期结果良好（以极少的生成token达到与强化学习相当的准确率），但其核心不对称性（教师拥有学生没有的特权信息）产生了致命缺陷——坍缩，即模型推理路径集合的渐进性收窄。目前文献中对这一现象命名不一、理解分散，缺乏统一的术语和分析框架。作者希望通过系统化梳理，为这一主流失败模式提供结构性理解。

Method: 这是一篇综述论文，不包含新实验。作者采用结构性分析框架，将坍缩分解为三个可调控的杠杆：(i)信号应用位置（token加权策略）；(ii)教师所见内容（特权信息的类型：参考解、计划、环境反馈等）；(iii)信号变化时机（教师动态和引导衰减）。论文限定在数学推理领域，因为该领域是OPSD的起源地且失败模式记录最完整。

Result: 文章的主要成就是提供了一个统一的共享词汇表，将不同论文中以不同名称描述的现象统一收编到"坍缩"这一概念之下；同时明确划分了该领域"已解决"和"仍有争议"的边界。早期OPSD结果虽与强化学习精度相当且token消耗更低，但坍缩问题已成为该领域的主导性失败模式。

Conclusion: 坍缩并非OPSD独有，但特权信息会加剧这一问题。论文通过三个杠杆的系统化分类，为理解和干预坍缩提供了结构性框架。其核心贡献是从方法结果转向结构化认知，为该领域的后续研究提供共同语言和分析基础。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a language model on its own generations while a teacher scores them token by token. It combines the dense supervision of imitation learning with the on-policy sampling of reinforcement learning. But it requires a second, larger model to act as teacher. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) removes that cost. The teacher is the model itself, conditioned on privileged information the student will not have at test time, such as a reference solution, a plan, or environment feedback. The teacher is no stronger than the student, only better informed. Early results were promising, with accuracy comparable to reinforcement learning at a fraction of the generated tokens. But the same asymmetry that produces the signal also biases it. One failure mode now dominates the field: collapse, the progressive narrowing of the set of reasoning paths the model can produce. Collapse is not specific to OPSD, though privileged information aggravates it. This review treats collapse as a symptom governed by three levers: (i) where the signal is applied, that is, how tokens are weighted; (ii) what the teacher is shown, that is, the nature of the privileged information; and (iii) when the signal changes, that is, the teacher's dynamics and the decay of guidance. We restrict our scope to mathematical reasoning, where the method originated and where its failure modes are best documented. We report no new experiments. The contribution is structural: a shared vocabulary for phenomena named differently across papers, and a clear line between what is settled and what is still disputed.

</details>


### [252] [A General-Purpose Molecular Foundation Model Transfers Across Diverse Olfactory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25893)
*Yikun Han,Yi Wang,Neil Mankodi,Stephen Yang,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过微调分子基础模型Uni-Mol2于单一嗅觉预测任务（GS-LF基准），评估其在四个下游任务（跨数据集气味描述符预测、有气味vs无气味分类、对映体评估、气味混合物区分）中的迁移表现，验证了"一次性训练、跨任务迁移"的机器嗅觉范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究分子基础模型在单一规范嗅觉任务上微调后，能否学习到可迁移到多种机器嗅觉问题的通用表征，以支持更高效、通用的嗅觉预测范式。

Method: 在GS-LF多标签气味描述符预测基准上微调Uni-Mol2，并在四种互补下游设置中进行零额外深度学习训练的评估：跨数据集气味描述符预测、有气味/无气味分类、对映体评估以及气味混合物判别性分析。

Result: 微调模型在GS-LF主基准上与最先进的嗅味专用基线相当或更优，并一致地迁移到所有下游评估任务。对映体分析表明三维分子表征能区分镜像分子，这是二维图模型无法做到的，但准确预测立体化学的感知后果仍有挑战。

Conclusion: 结果支持机器嗅觉的"一次性训练、跨任务迁移"范式，表明化学预训练的分子表征为可迁移的嗅觉预测提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Foundation models have transformed molecular property prediction, yet it remains unclear whether a molecular foundation model, fine-tuned on a single canonical olfactory prediction task, can learn representations that transfer across diverse machine olfaction problems. We investigate this question by fine-tuning Uni-Mol2 on the GS-LF benchmark for multi-label odor descriptor prediction and evaluating the resulting model, without additional deep-learning training, on four complementary downstream settings: cross-dataset odor descriptor prediction, odorous-versus-odorless classification, enantiomer evaluation, and odor mixture discriminability. The fine-tuned model matches or exceeds the performance of the state-of-the-art olfaction-specific baseline on the primary GS-LF benchmark and consistently transfers across these downstream evaluations. The enantiomer analysis further shows that three-dimensional molecular representations distinguish mirror-image molecules in a way that two-dimensional graph models fundamentally cannot, although accurately predicting the perceptual consequences of stereochemistry remains an open challenge. Together, these results support a train-once, transfer-across-tasks paradigm for machine olfaction and suggest that chemically pretrained molecular representations provide a strong foundation for transferable olfactory prediction.

</details>


### [253] [Towards A Unified Information Bottleneck Framework for Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25897)
*Xu Zheng,Zichuan Liu,Zhuomin Chen,Mayur Akewar,Janki Bhimani,Jason Liu,Mo Sha,Jingchao Ni,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于信息瓶颈原理的统一可解释性框架，将时间序列的归因解释与反事实解释整合到单一目标函数中，通过参数化变换网络同时生成忠实归因和稳定反事实，在合成与真实基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列解释方法分为归因解释和反事实解释两大类，但二者被独立研究：归因方法缺乏因果验证，反事实方法存在严重不稳定性，容易产生类似对抗攻击的噪声而非有意义的解释。作者从信息论角度揭示现有解释器易受平凡解和分布漂移的影响。

Method: 提出一个统一的目标函数，基于信息瓶颈(Information Bottleneck)原理将归因与反事实推理桥接到单一框架中，显式防止平凡解释和超出分布的反事实。基于该目标函数引入X-model框架，学习一个参数化变换网络来构建嵌入解释的实例：保留信息产生归因解释，受控的信息移除产生稳定的反事实解释。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上对照最先进基线进行评估，定量和定性结果表明该框架在生成忠实归因和稳定反事实解释方面一致优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 通过信息论视角统一两类解释范式是可行且有效的，信息瓶颈原理能够同时解决归因缺乏因果验证和反事实不稳定的问题，为时间序列可解释性提供了一个统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Explaining deep learning models operating on time series data is crucial in various applications that require transparent and interpretable insights into model behavior. {Existing explanation methods generally fall into two categories: attribution-based explanations, which identify the temporal regions most responsible for a prediction, and counterfactual explanations, which reveal how an input should be modified to alter the model's decision.} {Despite valuable insights, these two fields are largely studied independently. This disconnect leaves attribution methods lacking causal validation, while counterfactual methods suffer from severe instability, producing adversarial-like noise instead of meaningful explanations.} In this work, we revisit time-series explainability from an information-theoretic perspective and show that existing explainers are vulnerable to trivial solutions and distributional shifts. To address these limitations, we propose a unified objective function for explainable time series learning that bridges attribution and counterfactual reasoning within a single framework. Building upon the Information Bottleneck principle, our formulation explicitly prevents trivial explanations and out-of-distribution counterfactuals. {Based on this objective function, we introduce {\modelname}, a novel explanation framework that learns a parametric transformation network to construct explanation-embedded instances, where preserved information yields attribution explanations and controlled information removal produces stable counterfactual explanations.} We evaluate {\modelname} on synthetic and real-world benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines. Extensive quantitative and qualitative results show that {\modelname} consistently outperforms competing methods, yielding faithful attributions and stable counterfactual explanations.

</details>


### [254] [Forecasting Multiple Observables with SCROLL: Score-Trained Uncertainty for Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25898)
*Pavel Prochazka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务随机动力学预测方法：在共享骨干网络上为每个任务使用自由路由（free-routed）的最后一层信念（belief）来组合观测量的似然，从而避免手动调参平衡各任务损失。该方法在随机动力学系统上优于或持平于标准多任务基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动力学系统的预测需要同时输出多个观测量的似然（如未来状态、阈值事件、区域标签），而非单一数值。标准多任务方法需要手动或学习地平衡各任务损失权重，代价高昂且难以推广。此外，静态基准缺乏随机动力学系统所能提供的"可计算的预测方差真值"，因此需要一个能利用这一特性的新框架。

Method: 提出在共享骨干网络上，为每个任务构建"自由路由的最后一层信念"（free-routed last-layer beliefs），将观测量的似然组合起来。单位相关的损失缩放被吸收进似然参数中，并在同一次梯度更新中学习，无需额外调整任务权重。对均方差（homoscedastic）系统可恢复解析核，对异方差（heteroscedastic）系统则学习输入相关的方差。

Result: 在适定的同方差Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程上，学得的预测律恢复了解析核，与正确设定的基线持平。在异方差系统（随机Lorenz-63和真实空气质量数据）上，信念的输入相关方差具有辨识性：在状态和区域任务上取得最佳单次运行NLL，校准性能仅被NLL更差的基线匹配，且计算成本远低于调参网格。在真实数据序列上，状态边际在五个滚动起点上保持优势。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了通过将任务损失平衡内化到似然参数中，可以在随机动力学预测任务上以更低成本达到或超越传统多任务调参方法，特别是在异方差系统上展现出优势。

Abstract: Forecasting a stochastic dynamical system rarely means a single number: one wants several observables---future state, threshold event, regime label---each with its own likelihood. Standard multi-task recipes balance per-task losses, tuned or learned. We instead compose the observables' likelihoods in per-task free-routed last-layer beliefs on a shared backbone; this absorbs unit-dependent loss scaling into likelihood parameters learned in the same gradient pass. Stochastic dynamics supply what static benchmarks cannot: computable ground truth for the predictive variance. Results land where theory puts them: on the well-specified, homoscedastic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process the learned predictive law recovers the analytic kernel and correctly specified baselines tie. On heteroscedastic systems (stochastic Lorenz-63, real air-quality data) the belief's input-dependent variance separates: best single-run NLL on the state and regime tasks, calibration matched only by arms whose NLL it beats, at a fraction of the tuned grids' cost. On the real series the state margin holds across five rolling origins.

</details>


### [255] [How Much Rank Does LoRA Need? Rank-Error Bounds for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26052)
*Gerard Conangla Planes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务相关的理论框架，用于分析Transformer注意力中低秩适应（LoRA）更新在不同秩下的逼近误差。通过设定预训练注意力头、目标注意力函数和下游任务输入分布，作者为秩为r的query LoRA更新给出了期望KL误差的上下界，并扩展至多头和联合query/key更新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择LoRA更新的秩通常是一个经验性任务，缺乏理论指导。本文旨在为Transformer注意力中的LoRA秩选择提供任务依赖的理论基础，从而在理论上指导实践中秩的选择。

Method: 作者构建了一个理论分析框架：固定预训练注意力头、目标注意力函数及下游任务输入分布，推导秩r query LoRA更新的最小期望KL误差。通过尾部能量T_r刻画目标更新的谱特性，在可实现性、几何和矩条件下给出误差界。同时分析softmax饱和效应，并扩展至多头LoRA和联合query/key更新的分析。

Result: 证明了误差下界正比于ψ(‖d‖₂)（其中ψ(t)=min{t²,t}），以及无条件上界min{‖d‖₂²/4, √2‖d‖₂}。在更具体条件下，将最佳秩r误差界定在ψ(√T_r)的倍数与min{T_r/4, √2T_r}之间。还给出了目标Fisher界、无限制下界，并发现softmax饱和可使匹配注意力函数所需秩严格小于匹配有限logits所需秩。

Conclusion: 该理论提供了LoRA秩选择的任务依赖指导，揭示秩共享和query/key分解约束对逼近能力的影响，并表明通过softmax非线性，所需秩可被有效降低，为实践中秩选择的效率提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Choosing the rank of a low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update is usually an empirical task. In this paper, we provide a task-dependent theory of the approximation error achievable at each LoRA rank for Transformer attention. We fix a pretrained attention head, a target attention function, and a distribution over inputs from the downstream task, and bound the smallest expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) error achievable by a rank-$r$ query LoRA update. When target attention probabilities are bounded away from zero, we prove a lower bound of the error proportional to $ψ(\|d\|_2)$, where $d$ is the difference between candidate and target attention scores and $ψ(t)=\min\{t^2,t\}$. We also prove an unconditional upper bound $\min\{\|d\|_2^2/4,\sqrt2\|d\|_2\}$. Under explicit realizability, geometry, and moment conditions, we then bound the best rank-$r$ error between an explicit multiple of $ψ(\sqrt{T_r})$ and $\min\{T_r/4,\sqrt{2T_r}\}$, where $T_r$ is the downstream-weighted tail energy of the target update. We also provide target-Fisher bounds when candidate scores remain within a fixed range of the target scores, and an unrestricted lower bound when a subset of tokens carries most of the probability mass. These spectral bounds describe finite-score approximation. We then construct explicit families in which softmax saturation makes the rank required to match the attention function strictly smaller than the rank required to match the finite logits. Finally, we extend the analysis to fused multi-head LoRA and joint query/key updates, exposing the effects of rank sharing and query/key factorization constraints.

</details>


### [256] [ICON Decomposition: Multivariate Concept-Level Explanations of Deep Representations for Model Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26083)
*Roshan Prakash Rane,Marco Simnacher,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marc-Andre Schulz,Nys Tjade Siegel,Maximilian Dreyer,Frederik Pahde,Wojciech Samek,Sonja Greven,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICON分解方法，用于量化神经网络层中每个概念在考虑其他所有概念和结果后所解释的方差比例，从而更准确地识别模型真正依赖的概念，避免因概念间相关性而误判捷径学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于概念的可解释性方法逐一测试每个概念能否从网络层中解码，忽略了概念之间的相关性，容易将概念间的相关关系误认为模型对概念的依赖，从而错误识别捷径学习。

Method: ICON分解通过方差分解的方式，量化每个概念在控制其他所有概念和结果变量后所解释的层方差比例，从而剥离概念间的相关性，准确估计每个概念的独立贡献。

Result: 在具有已知真实标签的合成数据上，ICON比七种基线方法更准确地恢复概念重要性；在皮肤病变和脑成像模型上，成功隔离模型真正依赖的概念，量化未被任何给定概念解释的表征部分，并通过重训练和分布外测试验证了稀疏解释的有效性。

Conclusion: ICON分解提供了一种更可靠的概念归因方法，能够有效避免概念的共线性混淆，为深度学习模型中的捷径学习检测和概念级可解释性提供了更严谨的工具。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often exploit spurious associations in their training data, a failure known as shortcut learning. Concept-based explainability methods screen for shortcuts by testing whether concepts such as a patient's sex or scanner settings can be decoded from a network layer. Because each concept is evaluated in isolation, these methods can mistake correlations between concepts as evidence that the model uses them. We introduce ICON decomposition, which instead quantifies how much of a layer's variance each concept explains after accounting for all other concepts and the outcome. On synthetic data with known ground truth, ICON recovers concept importance more accurately than seven alternative baseline methods. On skin-lesion and brain-imaging models, it isolates the concepts on which a model genuinely relies, quantifies the representation unexplained by any of the supplied concepts, and yields sparse explanations that we validate by retraining and out-of-distribution testing.

</details>


### [257] [Quantum-Inspired Modeling of Driving Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25907)
*Mohammad Elayan,Omid Armantalab,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子启发的驾驶员行为表征框架，将驾驶员编码为演化的密度矩阵，统一表征行为不确定性、时间演化和上下文依赖性，在无监督条件下从I-24 MOTION数据集中恢复出自由流、过渡和拥堵三种可解释的驾驶模式，并能复现宏观交通现象（基本图和滞回环），同时为传统跟驰模型提供上下文相关参数，为自动驾驶提供周围驾驶员实时行为读取与短时预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统驾驶行为模型预先固定行为变量的交互方式和形式，超出该形式的行为被当作噪声处理，而灵活度足够的模型又往往丧失可解释性。驾驶行为具有异质性、上下文依赖性和时变性，需要一种既能捕捉复杂性又保持可解释性的统一表征框架。

Method: 采用量子力学中密度矩阵的概念，将每个驾驶员编码为随时间演化的密度矩阵，该表征同时具备连续性、概率性、上下文依赖性、历史依赖性，并能从数据中学习行为变量间的交互关系。在I-24 MOTION数据集上以无监督方式训练该框架。

Result: 框架恢复了三种可解释的驾驶画像，对应自由流、过渡和拥堵三种交通状态；这些画像捕捉了数据中的行为范围以及驾驶员在不同状态间的平滑过渡。同一表征还能复现宏观现象，与基本图一致并再现滞回环。该表征可为经典跟驰模型提供上下文相关参数，并为自动驾驶车辆提供周围驾驶员的实时行为读取和短期运动预测。

Conclusion: 该框架指向一种天然可解释且可信赖的交通建模方式，兼顾个体行为的复杂性与宏观现象的再现能力，并在实际应用中（如跟驰建模和自动驾驶）展现实用价值。相关开源工具包已在GitHub发布。

Abstract: Driver behavior is heterogeneous, context-dependent, and changes over time, and these properties shape the traffic phenomena we observe. Most models, however, fix in advance which behavioral variables interact and how. Behavior outside that form is absorbed as noise, while models flexible enough to capture it tend to lose interpretability. We introduce a quantum-inspired representation of driver behavior that combines properties usually treated separately or in part: it is continuous, probabilistic, context-dependent, history-dependent, and represents interactions among behavioral variables as learned from data. Each driver is encoded as an evolving density matrix, providing a unified representation of behavioral uncertainty, temporal evolution, and context-dependent behavioral variation. Trained without supervision on the I-24 MOTION dataset, the framework recovers three interpretable driving profiles representing three regimes: free flow, transition, and congestion. The profiles capture the behavioral range of the data and the smooth transitions drivers make between regimes as conditions change. The same representation also reproduces known macroscopic phenomena, aligning with the fundamental diagram and reproducing hysteresis loops. We also show how the representation supports practical use: it supplies context-dependent parameters to classical car-following models, and gives an autonomous vehicle a live behavioral read of the surrounding drivers with a short-horizon forecast of their motion. The framework points toward models of traffic that are interpretable and trustworthy by construction. We release an open-source toolkit on GitHub (https://github.com/mselayan/quantum-driver-representation) spanning data processing, training, inference, and analysis.

</details>


### [258] [When Pruning Meets Interpretability: Preserving Sparse Autoencoder Robustness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25941)
*Suchit Gupte,Xueru Zhang,Mohammad Mahdi Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了后验模型剪枝对稀疏自编码器（SAE）可靠性的影响，理论上表明剪枝对SAE的影响由扰动能量（协方差加权范数）决定，发现幅度剪枝因忽略激活几何而损害SAE功能，而Wanda和SparseGPT等激活感知方法更稳健。同时揭示所有剪枝方法均存在中间层更敏感的结构性弱点，据此提出层间稀疏度分配策略，在四个模型架构上验证了理论发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE广泛用于解释LLM内部表示，但其在后验模型压缩下的可靠性尚未被充分理解。理解剪枝如何影响SAE行为对于确保模型压缩后仍能保持可解释性至关重要。

Method: 从理论上将剪枝对固定SAE的影响形式化为由协方差加权范数（扰动能量）控制的量；对比幅度剪枝与激活感知方法（Wanda、SparseGPT）在此框架下的差异；基于中间层更敏感的结构性发现，提出层间稀疏度分配策略。

Result: 理论推导验证了扰动能量是该问题的核心度量；实验表明幅度剪枝显著扭曲表示空间、损害SAE功能，而激活感知方法更稳健；所有方法在中间层更脆弱；所提出的层间稀疏度分配策略在相同平均剪枝稀疏度下获得更低困惑度，并在四种模型架构上得到验证。

Conclusion: 提出以扰动能量为统一视角理解剪枝对SAE的影响，揭示了现有方法的局限与优势，并利用层敏感性差异设计了更优的稀疏度分配方案，为可解释性和模型压缩的协同提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret the internal representations of large language models (LLMs), yet their reliability under post-hoc model compression remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of how pruning affects SAE behavior and theoretically show that, for a fixed SAE, its impact is governed by perturbation energy, a covariance-weighted norm. This perspective exposes a key limitation of magnitude pruning: by ignoring activation geometry, it distorts the learned representation space and degrades SAE functionality. Activation-aware methods such as Wanda and SparseGPT, in contrast, implicitly control perturbation energy and are therefore substantially more robust at preserving SAE behavior. We further reveal a consistent structural vulnerability across all pruning methods: middle layers are significantly more sensitive to pruning than early or late layers. Guided by this insight, we propose a layer-wise sparsity allocation strategy, achieving lower perplexity under the same average pruning sparsity. Experiments across four model architectures validate our theoretical findings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/osu-srml/sae-robustness-under-pruning/tree/main.

</details>


### [259] [Spectral Allocation: Why Muon Outperforms Adam, and How to Improve Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25990)
*Xiaodong Wu,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Philip Woodland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过频谱探针分析揭示了正交优化器（如Muon）加速LLM预训练的机制：损失景观在奇异方向上呈现各向异性——头部（head）在稳定边缘（Edge-of-Stability）运行需要较小步长，而主体（bulk）可容忍较大步长。基于此发现提出Spectral-Aware Muon（SAMuon），通过静态频谱先验放大主体步长，在124M到1B参数的模型上相比Muon节省13.3%-24.0%的训练token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Muon等正交优化器相比Adam能大幅加速LLM预训练，但其内在机制仍未被完全理解。作者希望通过频谱分析揭示损失景观的结构，从而解释不同优化器性能差异的原因，并据此改进优化器设计。

Method: 1）提出样本外频谱探针分析：在真实训练轨迹的检查点沿动量缓冲区的奇异方向分解，估计各方向在留出数据上的损失最优步长。2）发现频谱轮廓呈各向异性但稳定，头部需小步长而主体可大步长。3）据此设计SAMuon：保持头部在Muon尺度，用静态频谱先验放大主体步长。提供两个变体：完整SAMuon使用低秩随机SVD，SAMuon-lite使用两水平秩一幂迭代近似。两种方法均不增加持久优化器状态或显著额外FLOPs。

Result: 在124M到1B参数的"modded-nanogpt"模型上，SAMuon和SAMuon-lite在所有评估的模型规模和批次大小配置中均优于调优的AdamW和Muon（Scion实现）基线。SAMuon相比Muon节省13.3%-24.0%的训练token达到相同验证损失，SAMuon-lite保留大部分收益且墙钟开销接近为零。理想化的精确白化版本在标准假设下保留Muon的渐近收敛率。

Conclusion: 频谱分配视角统一解释了Muon优于Adam优于SGD的差异，同时揭示了Muon均匀缩放策略的局限——仍未能充分利用主体的容量。SAMuon通过静态频谱先验弥补了这一不足，以近乎零开销显著提升训练效率。

Abstract: Orthogonal optimisers such as Muon can substantially accelerate large language model pretraining relative to Adam, yet the mechanism remains incompletely understood. We investigate this through an out-of-sample spectral probing analysis of Transformer loss landscapes. At checkpoints along real training trajectories, we decompose each momentum buffer into its singular directions and estimate the loss-optimal step size along each direction on held-out data. The resulting spectral profile is anisotropic yet stable across batches and training stages, and consistent across the optimisers and model scales: a volatile head operating at the Edge-of-Stability supports a much smaller step size than the tolerant bulk, which permits substantially larger steps. This profile provides a unified spectral allocation account of why Muon outperforms Adam, which outperforms SGD. It also exposes a limitation of Muon's uniform scaling: it still underutilises the bulk. Guided by this finding, we introduce Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon), which holds the head at the Muon scale and amplifies the bulk using a static spectral prior. We provide two variants: the complete SAMuon follows the measured profile using a low-rank randomised SVD and the simplified SAMuon-lite uses a two-level approximation via rank-one power iteration. Neither method adds persistent optimiser state or notable extra FLOPs beyond Muon at scale, and the idealised exact-whitening versions of both retain Muon's asymptotic convergence rate under standard assumptions. Across "modded-nanogpt" models from 124M to 1B parameters, both variants outperform tuned AdamW and Muon (Scion implementation) baselines in all evaluated model-scale and batch-size configurations. SAMuon requires 13.3% to 24.0% fewer training tokens to reach the same validation loss as Muon, while SAMuon-lite retains most of this gain with near-zero wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [260] [DualOPSD: Adaptive Privileged Teachers for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26019)
*Yutong Chen,Guangfu Guo,Zhichao Xu,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualOPSD，一种非对称交替框架，使在线策略自蒸馏中的教师和学生策略都动态适应。学生在教师指导下学习后，教师沿同一学生轨迹向更新后的学生分布移动，从而提供响应式监督而无需额外rollout。在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，AIME 2024/2025和HMMT 2025上avg@12分别比OPSD提升23.61、13.89和10.00点，并减少截断现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OPSD方法中特权教师模型在训练过程中保持固定，但学生的分布和输出风格会随训练变化，导致教师提供的监督信号逐渐与学生不匹配，降低了自蒸馏的有效性。

Method: 提出DualOPSD非对称交替框架：学生先从特权教师处学习，随后教师沿同一学生轨迹向更新后的学生分布移动以适配学习者。该过程无需额外rollout，使后续监督能响应学生的动态变化。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，DualOPSD相比OPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上avg@12分别提升23.61、13.89和10.00点。在1.7B和4B规模下验证了精度提升依赖模型规模。三个规模下均减少截断，4B诊断显示教师与学生之间双向KL散度更低。

Conclusion: DualOPSD通过让教师动态适应学生分布，克服了固定教师监督信号失配的问题，在多个基准上显著超越OPSD，同时减少截断并降低双向KL散度，证明了交替适应策略的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.

</details>


### [261] [Robust CurveMoE: Multi-Norm Adversarial Defense for Mixture-of-Experts Models via Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26043)
*Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Robust CurveMoE，一种高效的多范数对抗防御混合专家框架，通过低损耗路径连接不同扰动范数特化的模型，并仅对关键层进行专家化，同时引入贡献引导的部分更新来降低曲线构建成本，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多种范数对抗防御方法在单一参数配置下优化相互竞争的鲁棒性目标，导致训练成本高且鲁棒性之间出现不利的权衡。

Method: 1) 从鲁棒性约束的曲线位置推导出干净和范数特化的专家；2) 仅选择性专家化关键层，其余参数在路由路径间共享；3) 提出贡献引导的部分更新，利用初始化梯度分数选取关键曲线参数，以降低曲线构建成本；4) 对部分与完整曲线优化的目标差距进行理论分析。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上使用WideResNet和Vision Transformer的实验表明，Robust CurveMoE在干净精度、范数特化精度和Union精度上均一致优于MSD和ERMC，Union精度分别比最强基线提升2.37和2.13个百分点。

Conclusion: Robust CurveMoE通过曲线连接和选择性专家化有效解决了多范数对抗防御中的训练成本与鲁棒性权衡问题，消融实验验证了部分更新、选择性专家化和鲁棒性约束专家选择的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-norm adversarial defense aims to protect neural networks against perturbations defined by different norm constraints, but existing methods typically optimize competing robustness objectives within a single parameter configuration, leading to substantial training cost and unfavorable robustness trade-offs. We propose Robust CurveMoE, an efficient mixture-of-experts framework that connects models specialized for different perturbation norms through a low-loss path and exploits the complementary robustness profiles of models along this path. Robust CurveMoE derives clean and norm-specialized experts from robustness-constrained curve locations and selectively expertizes only influential layers, while sharing the remaining parameters across routing paths. To further reduce curve-construction cost, we introduce contribution-guided partial updating, which selects influential curve parameters using initialization-based gradient scores. We also theoretically bound the objective gap between partial and full curve optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 with WideResNet and Vision Transformer architectures show that Robust CurveMoE consistently improves clean, norm-specific, and Union accuracy over MSD and ERMC. In particular, it improves Union accuracy by 2.37 and 2.13 percentage points over the strongest baseline on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100, respectively. Extensive ablations further validate the effectiveness of partial updating, selective expertization, and robustness-constrained expert selection.

</details>


### [262] [Group-Shared Low-Rank Approximation for Mobile-Efficient Pointwise Convolutions in Large-Kernel CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26069)
*Hao Luo,Yiting Yang,Wenyi Zhao,Man Jiang,Zhijun Lin,Ghulam Mohiuddin,Ting Jiang,Kunming Luo,Zihao Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Guoqing Wang,Wei Dong,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Channel Group-Shared (CGS)低秩近似方法，针对大核CNN在边缘设备部署时点卷积参数占比过高（>87%）的存储瓶颈问题，通过SVD分解和跨通道组共享投影矩阵的策略，显著压缩参数规模，使大核CNN模型（RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK）能够在资源受限的边缘设备上高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大核CNN通过扩大感受野带来卓越性能，但参数随核大小呈二次增长，严重阻碍边缘部署。现有方法聚焦于压缩深度卷积，却忽视了占参数总量>87%的点卷积才是主要部署瓶颈。在手机等仅4-12GB RAM的设备上，模型存储和加载成本成为关键约束。

Method: 提出基于SVD的CGS参数共享策略：构造与SVD分解同构的结构化低秩范式，包含跨通道组共享的高参数开销下/上投影矩阵，以及通道组专属的低参数开销可扩展对角矩阵。通过组共享设计实现显著的参数缩减。

Result: 实验表明，将CGS应用于RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK等大核CNN模型，能在保持竞争力的性能的同时大幅降低存储成本，并缓解存储约束、降低内存带宽压力、减少模型加载延迟，实现边缘设备上的可行部署。

Conclusion: CGS通过针对性解决点卷积参数瓶颈，成功弥合了高性能视觉模型与边缘设备实际部署之间的鸿沟，为大核CNN在资源受限设备上的应用提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Large-kernel Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) deliver remarkable performance in vision tasks by significantly expanding receptive fields, yet their quadratic parameter growth critically impedes storage-efficient edge deployment. While existing efficient architectures adopt parameter-efficient depthwise separable convolution backbones that leverage techniques like low-rank approximation and weight sharing to compress depthwise convolutions, we identify a critical oversight: pointwise convolutions dominate parameter volume (>87% in models like RepLKNet-31B) and constitute the primary deployment bottleneck on resource-constrained edge devices. This results in prohibitive storage costs and severe memory-loading constraints on resource-limited devices (e.g., smartphones with 4-12 GB Random Access Memory (RAM)). To overcome this, we propose Channel Group-Shared (CGS) low-rank approximation, a novel Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based parameter-sharing strategy. CGS constructs a structured low-rank paradigm isomorphic to SVD decomposition, comprising shared (high-parameter-cost) down/up-projection matrices across channel groups within a layer and channel-group-specific (low-parameter-cost) scalable diagonal matrices. This group-sharing design achieves significant parameter reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that large-kernel CNNs (RepLKNet, ConvNeXt, SLaK) enhanced with CGS strike an empirically favorable balance between competitive performance and substantially reduced storage costs. Crucially, by alleviating storage constraints, reducing memory bandwidth pressure during loading, and minimizing model loading latency, CGS enables the feasible deployment of pre-trained large-kernel CNN models on edge devices, thereby bridging the gap between high-performance vision models and practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [263] [TraceML: An Empirical Analysis of Human-Agent Planning in Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26086)
*Jiarui Yan,Weiwei Sun,Sijie Li,Wenhan Li,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TraceML数据集，通过版本级模式配对人类和智能体在同一竞赛中的工作轨迹（134个竞赛、4465个人类轨迹、430个配对智能体轨迹），揭示大语言模型在自主机器学习开发中的差距根源：人类专家会多样化切换数据、验证、模型和集成工作并重新审视旧方法，而智能体陷入狭窄循环（Codex反复调权重集成，MLEvolve原地变异模型），不会像人类那样转向或重开废弃工作。从人类实践中提炼的规划提示虽能提升得分和部分行为，但努力分布仍呈智能体形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结果导向基准只能记录大语言模型在自主机器学习开发中与人类的差距，却因只评价最终提交而丢弃开发过程，无法揭示差距的成因。需要一种能分析整个开发过程的基准来理解差距本质。

Method: 构建TraceML数据集，采用版本级模式配对人类与智能体的工作：收集134个竞赛中的4465个人类Kaggle轨迹，其中7个竞赛由两种智能体框架（Codex、MLEvolve）共同完成，得到430个配对人类轨迹和207个智能体轨迹。每个代码版本都带有得分、时间戳、动作标签、意图、编辑大小和得分效果等元数据，用于对比分析与差距诊断。

Result: 人类专家会交替进行数据工作、验证、模型变更和集成，并会返回此前搁置的方法；而两类智能体都陷入狭窄循环——Codex反复集成重加权和调优提交、MLEvolve在原地变异模型，两者既不以人类频率转向，也不重开放弃的工作。从人类实践提炼的简短规划提示能推动其命名的行为向人类形态靠拢并提升得分，但努力分布整体仍保持智能体形态，说明指令只能弥合可归结为指令的那部分差距。

Conclusion: 通过过程级分析可将大语言模型与人类在自主机器学习开发上的差距具体化：差距根源在于智能体的策略探索范围过于狭窄，缺乏人类式的多样化工作切换与旧方法重访能力。规划提示能部分改善但无法完全消除差距，仍有非指令可归因的部分。数据集、模式、标注器和提取流程已开源。

Abstract: Large language models write correct code for isolated problems but remain far weaker at autonomous machine-learning development, where an agent must revise data pipelines, models, and validation over hours of feedback, and on most competitions still finishes below strong human competitors. Outcome-based benchmarks record this gap but not its cause, because they grade the final submission and discard the development process behind it. We introduce TraceML, which pairs human and agent work on the same competitions under one version-level schema: 4,465 human Kaggle trajectories across 134 competitions, seven of which are also worked by two agent scaffolds, giving 430 paired human and 207 agent trajectories. Every code version carries its score, its timestamp, and labels for the action taken, its intent, the edit size, and the score effect. Read this way, the gap becomes concrete. Experts alternate data work, validation, model changes, and ensembling, and return to approaches they had set aside. Each agent scaffold instead collapses into a narrow loop: Codex spends its steps re-weighting ensembles and tuning submissions, MLEvolve mutates its model in place, and neither pivots at the human rate nor reopens abandoned work. A short planning prompt distilled from human practice moves the behaviors it names toward the human profile and lifts scores, but the effort profile stays agent-shaped: instruction closes only the part of the gap that reduces to instructions. We release the corpus, the schema, the labelers, and the extraction pipeline at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jerryyan/TraceML.

</details>


### [264] [Agentic Autoresearch for Cell-Edge Power Control: Radically Redefining the Researcher's Role](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26093)
*Ahmad Khan,Akram Bin Sediq,Sara Azadegi Naeini,Raviraj S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种将无线资源管理机器学习算法的整个设计层（架构、损失函数、训练方案）完全交给自主AI编码代理的方法。代理通过autoresearch协议自主搜索设计，在固定预算下自动编辑和评估训练脚本。在81次无人值守实验中，代理达到99.5%的参考性能，推理成本降低约600倍，并发现了可证明的结构而非调优常量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，为无线资源管理设计机器学习算法需要大量人工劳动——架构、损失函数和训练配方都需手动指定。本文的动机是证明这一设计层可以完全让渡给自主智能体，从而大幅减少人工干预，实现自动化算法设计。

Method: 采用autoresearch协议：AI编码代理编辑训练脚本、运行固定预算实验，并根据单一不可变指标决定保留或丢弃修改。代理拥有对架构族、输入表示、输出参数化、损失函数和任务采样律的完全设计权限。研究目标设定为多小区网络中总和最小百分位速率功率控制（非凸、非光滑、强NP难问题）。通过哈希固定评估器、强制推理契约和预注册证伪器确保结果可信。

Result: 在81次无人值守实验、26小时中，代理在一次固定成本推理中达到收敛的最小化-最大化参考值的99.5%，推理成本降低约600倍，缩小了与首个可用架构94%的差距，且一组参数适用于所有网络规模和百分位目标。发现的可证明结构：在最小百分位处，输出参数化重现了精确的最大-最小最优分配。

Conclusion: 本文证明无线资源管理算法设计的整个流程可以完全自动化，自主代理不仅能达到接近传统参考算法的性能，还能以更低推理成本发现具有可证明数学结构的方法，而非仅仅调优超参数，展示了自动化算法发现的潜力。

Abstract: Designing machine learning algorithms for wireless resource management is labour-intensive: the architecture, the loss function and the training recipe are all specified by hand. We demonstrate that this design layer can be surrendered to an autonomous agent in its entirety. We adopt the autoresearch protocol, in which an AI coding agent edits a training script, runs a fixed-budget experiment, and retains or discards the change according to a single immutable metric. We grant the agent authority over the architecture family, the input representation, the output parameterization, the loss function and the task-sampling law, and set it a target chosen for its difficulty: sum-least-percentile-rate power control across a multicell network. The formulation targets cell-edge throughput and is non-convex, non-smooth and strongly NP-hard away from its max-min vertex. Safeguards render the results trustworthy: a hash-pinned evaluator, an enforced inference contract and a pre-registered falsifier per experiment. In eighty-one unattended experiments over twenty-six hours, the agent reached $99.5\%$ of a converged minorization-maximization reference in one fixed-cost inference pass, at roughly $600\times$ lower inference cost, closing $94\%$ of the gap from its first working architecture, with one parameter set serving every network size and percentile target. It recovered provable structure rather than tuned constants: the output parameterization it discovered reproduces the exact max-min-optimal allocation at the minimum percentile, for every value of the trained weights.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的自动多粒度图(Level-of-Graphs, LoGs)构建方法，将建筑平面图转化为结构化图表示。以147个全球高校图书馆平面图为案例，VLM生成的图与人工标注高度一致（节点匹配率≥92%），其中中粒度图在节点级区域预测上表现最佳（Macro F1=0.647），粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估上最有效（Spearman ρ=0.610）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图蕴含丰富的功能空间关系知识，可用于设计检索、知识推理和BIM增强，但自动构建任务自适应的图表示对公共建筑而言仍具挑战性，缺乏可扩展、免标注的结构化信息提取方法。

Method: 定义多粒度Level-of-Graphs (LoGs)；基于VLM自动构建LoG，包括节点识别、边推理、文本解析和图粗化四个步骤；通过真实任务系统评估VLM生成表征的性能。

Result: 147个图书馆平面图实验中，VLM生成图与人工标注一致（节点匹配率≥92%，生成三个LoG耗时509.3秒/平面图）；中粒度图在节点预测最优（Macro F1=0.647，为细粒度复杂度的65%），粗粒度图在图级质量评估最优（ρ=0.610，为细粒度复杂度的16%）。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了可扩展、免标注的平面图结构化信息提取，将设计图转换为知识表征，推进了设计信息学并增强了建筑全生命周期中设计信息的利用率。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [266] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMGUIIDE方法，将用户上下文结构化为类型化、领域特定的"Sims"块，并用程序性示例进行约束，同时构建SIMBENCH基准测试套件（47个偏好条件规划任务）。实验证明程序性grounded的Sims在GPT-4o上优于RAG（+7.9偏好遵从点），且该优势在100个τ-bench任务上复现。参数量级上，任务匹配的LoRA微调优于未适应基座模型（+12.8 ROUGE-L），按Sim类型而非用户身份路由适配器额外提升7.3点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化AI代理将用户视为单一实体（扁平轮廓拼接进提示词），当同一用户在不同生活场景中持有冲突优先级时该方法失效。核心问题不在于代理缺乏用户信息，而在于用户表示的格式决定了代理能否利用这些信息。作者主张表示格式而非表示内容是首要设计变量。

Method: SIMGUIDE方法：1) 将用户上下文结构化为类型化、领域特定的"Sims"块；2) 每个约束用来自过去决策的程序性示例进行grounded。评估框架SIMBENCH：47个偏好条件规划任务，正确计划取决于哪个用户上下文处于激活状态。同时进行参数量级实验，使用任务匹配的LoRA微调和按Sim类型路由适配器。

Result: 1) 纯声明式Sims约束不比RAG好；2) 程序性grounded的Sims在GPT-4o上超越RAG（+7.9偏好遵从点，p=0.013）；3) 该优势在100个τ-bench任务上于GPT-4o和Claude Sonnet 4.5均复现（p≤0.023）；4) 任务匹配的LoRA微调比未适应基座提升12.8 ROUGE-L点；5) 按Sim类型而非用户身份路由适配器额外增加7.3点，且对28%路由错误具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 用户表示的格式（而非内容）是决定个性化AI代理能否有效利用用户信息的第一阶设计变量。结构化的、程序性grounded的用户上下文表示在提示词工程和参数量级两个层面上都显著优于传统的扁平化表示和RAG方法。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $τ$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>


### [267] [Reliable LLM-Powered Decision Engines for Large-Scale Supply Chain Operations: Architecture, Safety, and Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24889)
*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM驱动的决策引擎（LLM-DE），将大语言模型与数学优化、概率预测和安全约束决策过滤相结合，用于大规模供应链管理，实现端到端的智能决策（需求预测、库存优化、运输路由和中断缓解），兼顾智能性、安全性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则和纯优化的供应链系统难以应对高度不确定、动态且易中断的现代供应链环境；而日益丰富的异构数据源（交易信号、非结构化中断报告）为构建能同时推理、适应和优化的智能系统提供了契机。

Method: 提出混合架构LLM-DE，核心是结合四个组件：大语言模型（负责语义推理）、数学优化（提供性能保证）、概率预测（处理不确定性）以及安全约束决策过滤（保障决策安全性）。该框架支持端到端决策流程，覆盖需求预测、库存优化、运输路由和中断缓解等环节。

Result: 实验结果表明，将基于语言的推理与优化和形式化约束相结合，能够产生既更智能又更安全且更具可扩展性的供应链决策，相较于纯数据驱动或启发式方案具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了新的架构、完整的算法流水线以及基于数学构建的运营决策系统公式，为下一代智能供应链基础设施奠定了务实且理论基础，使其能够在不确定性和大规模复杂性面前可靠运行。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions.
